# KTM 450 SX-F 2021: aftermarket parts

5285 listings whose stated fitment names this bike: 491 researched parts (with what they change and rider findings), 1070 dealer and retailer listings specific to this bike, and 3724 universal retailer listings (oil, grips, batteries and the like, counted below; every listing is in [parts.csv](../parts.csv)). Research candidates: check fitment on the maker's page before relying on a row. Back to [the family catalog](../CATALOG.md).
